# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-27)

## Corpus Check
- 6225 files · ~14,976,360 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 409 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .log 276, (none) 41, .css 16)

## Summary
- 70898 nodes · 209661 edges · 1492 communities (1350 shown, 142 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8723 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `b23b19d6`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
